@34 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he looked]{strong="G4017" x-morph="robinson: V-AMP-NSM 2"} [round about]{strong="G2945" x-morph="robinson: N-DSM 3"} [on them which sat]{strong="G2521" x-morph="robinson: V-PNP-APM 7"} [about]{strong="G4012" x-morph="robinson: PREP 5"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 6"}, [and said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3S 8"}, [Behold my mother and my brethren!]{.wj} 
@35 [For whosoever shall do the will of God, the same is my brother, and my sister, and mother.]{.wj} 

# 4 
@1 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he began]{strong="G756" x-morph="robinson: V-ADI-3S 3"} [again]{strong="G3825" x-morph="robinson: ADV 2"} [to teach]{strong="G1321" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 4"} [by]{strong="G3844" x-morph="robinson: PREP 5"} [the sea side]{strong="G3588 G2281" x-morph="robinson: T-ASF robinson: N-ASF 6 7"}: [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 8"} [there was gathered]{strong="G4863" x-morph="robinson: V-API-3S 9"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 10"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 11"} [a]{strong="G3793" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 12"} [great]{strong="G4183" x-morph="robinson: A-NSM 13"} [multitude]{strong="G3793" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 12"}, [so that]{strong="G5620" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 14"} [he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 15"} [entered]{strong="G1684" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAP-ASM 16"} [into]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 17"} [a ship]{strong="G3588 G4143" x-morph="robinson: T-ASN robinson: N-ASN 18 19"}, [and sat]{strong="G2521" x-morph="robinson: V-PNN 20"} [in]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 21"} [the sea]{strong="G3588 G2281" x-morph="robinson: T-DSF robinson: N-DSF 22 23"}; [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 24"} [the]{strong="G3588 G3793" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 26 27"} [whole]{strong="G3956" x-morph="robinson: A-NSM 25"} [multitude]{strong="G3793" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 27"} [was]{strong="G2258" x-morph="robinson: V-IXI-3S 34"} [by]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 28"} [the sea]{strong="G3588 G2281" x-morph="robinson: T-ASF robinson: N-ASF 29 30"} [on]{strong="G1909" x-morph="robinson: PREP 31"} [the land]{strong="G3588 G1093" x-morph="robinson: T-GSF robinson: N-GSF 32 33"}. 
@2 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he taught]{strong="G1321" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-APM 3"} [many things]{strong="G4183" x-morph="robinson: A-APN 6"} [by]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [parables]{strong="G3850" x-morph="robinson: N-DPF 5"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 7"} [said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 8"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 9"} [in]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 10"} [his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 13"} [doctrine]{strong="G3588 G1322" x-morph="robinson: T-DSF robinson: N-DSF 11 12"}, 
@3 [Hearken; Behold, there went out a sower to sow:]{.wj} 
@4 [And it came to pass, as he sowed, some fell by the way side, and the fowls of the air came and devoured it up.]{.wj} 
@5 [And some fell on stony ground, where it had not much earth; and immediately it sprang up, because it had no depth of earth:]{.wj} 
@6 [But when the sun was up, it was scorched; and because it had no root, it withered away.]{.wj} 
@7 [And some fell among thorns, and the thorns grew up, and choked it, and it yielded no fruit.]{.wj} 
@8 [And other fell on good ground, and did yield fruit that sprang up and increased; and brought forth, some thirty, and some sixty, and some an hundred.]{.wj} 
@9 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [He that hath ears to hear, let him hear.]{.wj} 
@10 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [when]{strong="G3753" x-morph="robinson: ADV 1"} [he was]{strong="G1096" x-morph="robinson: V-2ADI-3S 3"} [alone]{strong="G2651" x-morph="robinson: ADV 4"}, [they that were about]{strong="G4012" x-morph="robinson: PREP 8"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 9"} [with]{strong="G4862" x-morph="robinson: PREP 10"} [the twelve]{strong="G1427" x-morph="robinson: A-NUI 12"} [asked]{strong="G2065" x-morph="robinson: V-AAI-3P 5"} [of him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 6"} [the parable]{strong="G3588 G3850" x-morph="robinson: T-ASF robinson: N-ASF 13 14"}. 
@11 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Unto you it is given to know the mystery of the kingdom of God: but unto them that are without, all these things are done in parables:]{.wj} 
@12 [That seeing they may see, and not perceive; and hearing they may hear, and not understand; lest at any time they should be converted, and their sins should be forgiven them.]{.wj} 
@13 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Know ye not this parable? and how then will ye know all parables?]{.wj} 

@14 [The sower soweth the word.]{.wj} 
@15 [And these are they by the way side, where the word is sown; but when they have heard, Satan cometh immediately, and taketh away the word that was sown in their hearts.]{.wj} 
@16 [And these are they likewise which are sown on stony ground; who, when they have heard the word, immediately receive it with gladness;]{.wj} 
@17 [And have no root in themselves, and so endure but for a time: afterward, when affliction or persecution ariseth for the word’s sake, immediately they are offended.]{.wj} 
@18 [And these are they which are sown among thorns; such as hear the word,]{.wj} 
@19 [And the cares of this world, and the deceitfulness of riches, and the lusts of other things entering in, choke the word, and it becometh unfruitful.]{.wj} 
@20 [And these are they which are sown on good ground; such as hear the word, and receive it, and bring forth fruit, some thirtyfold, some sixty, and some an hundred.]{.wj} 

@21 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Is a candle brought to be put under a bushel, or under a bed? and not to be set on a candlestick?]{.wj} 
@22 [For there is nothing hid, which shall not be manifested; neither was any thing kept secret, but that it should come abroad.]{.wj} 
@23 [If any man have ears to hear, let him hear.]{.wj} 
@24 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Take heed what ye hear: with what measure ye mete, it shall be measured to you: and unto you that hear shall more be given.]{.wj} 
@25 [For he that hath, to him shall be given: and he that hath not, from him shall be taken even that which he hath.]{.wj} 

@26 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"}, [So is the kingdom of God, as if a man should cast seed into the ground;]{.wj} 
@27 [And should sleep, and rise night and day, and the seed should spring and grow up, he knoweth not how.]{.wj} 
@28 [For the earth bringeth forth fruit of herself; first the blade, then the ear, after that the full corn in the ear.]{.wj} 
@29 [But when the fruit is brought forth, immediately he putteth in the sickle, because the harvest is come.]{.wj} 

@30 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"}, [Whereunto shall we liken the kingdom of God? or with what comparison shall we compare it?]{.wj} 
@31 [It is like a grain of mustard seed, which, when it is sown in the earth, is less than all the seeds that be in the earth:]{.wj} 
@32 [But when it is sown, it groweth up, and becometh greater than all herbs, and shooteth out great branches; so that the fowls of the air may lodge under the shadow of it.]{.wj}

@6 [He answered]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-AOP-NSM 3"} [and]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 4"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 5"}, [Well hath Esaias prophesied of you hypocrites, as it is written, This people honoureth me with their lips, but their heart is far from me.]{.wj} 
@7 [Howbeit in vain do they worship me, teaching for doctrines the commandments of men.]{.wj} 
@8 [For laying aside the commandment of God, ye hold the tradition of men, as the washing of pots and cups: and many other such like things ye do.]{.wj} 
@9 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Full well ye reject the commandment of God, that ye may keep your own tradition.]{.wj} 
@10 [For Moses said, Honour thy father and thy mother; and, Whoso curseth father or mother, let him die the death:]{.wj} 
@11 [But ye say, If a man shall say to his father or mother, It is Corban, that is to say, a gift, by whatsoever thou mightest be profited by me; he shall be free.]{.wj} 
@12 [And ye suffer him no more to do ought for his father or his mother;]{.wj} 
@13 [Making the word of God of none effect through your tradition, which ye have delivered: and many such like things do ye.]{.wj} 

@14 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [when he had called]{strong="G4341" x-morph="robinson: V-ADP-NSM 2"} [all]{strong="G3956" x-morph="robinson: A-ASM 3"} [the people]{strong="G3588 G3793" x-morph="robinson: T-ASM robinson: N-ASM 4 5"} unto him, [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 6"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 7"}, [Hearken unto me every one of you, and understand:]{.wj} 
@15 [There is nothing from without a man, that entering into him can defile him: but the things which come out of him, those are they that defile the man.]{.wj} 
@16 [If any man have ears to hear, let him hear.]{.wj} 
@17 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [when]{strong="G3753" x-morph="robinson: ADV 2"} [he was entered]{strong="G1525" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 3"} [into]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [the house]{strong="G3624" x-morph="robinson: N-ASM 5"} [from]{strong="G575" x-morph="robinson: PREP 6"} [the people]{strong="G3588 G3793" x-morph="robinson: T-GSM robinson: N-GSM 7 8"}, [his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 13"} [disciples]{strong="G3588 G3101" x-morph="robinson: T-NPM robinson: N-NPM 11 12"} [asked]{strong="G1905" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3P 9"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 10"} [concerning]{strong="G4012" x-morph="robinson: PREP 14"} [the parable]{strong="G3588 G3850" x-morph="robinson: T-GSF robinson: N-GSF 15 16"}.

Do ye not perceive, that whatsoever thing from without entereth into the man, it cannot defile him;]{.wj} 
@19 [Because it entereth not into his heart, but into the belly, and goeth out into the draught, purging all meats?]{.wj} 
@20 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 1"}, [That which cometh out of the man, that defileth the man.]{.wj} 
@21 [For from within, out of the heart of men, proceed evil thoughts, adulteries, fornications, murders,]{.wj} 
@22 [Thefts, covetousness, wickedness, deceit, lasciviousness, an evil eye, blasphemy, pride, foolishness:]{.wj}

@40 [For he that is not against us is on our part.]{.wj} 
@41 [For whosoever shall give you a cup of water to drink in my name, because ye belong to Christ, verily I say unto you, he shall not lose his reward.]{.wj} 
@42 [And whosoever shall offend one of these little ones that believe in me, it is better for him that a millstone were hanged about his neck, and he were cast into the sea.]{.wj} 
@43 [And if thy hand offend thee, cut it off: it is better for thee to enter into life maimed, than having two hands to go into hell, into the fire that never shall be quenched:]{.wj} 
@44 [Where their worm dieth not, and the fire is not quenched.]{.wj} 
@45 [And if thy foot offend thee, cut it off: it is better for thee to enter halt into life, than having two feet to be cast into hell, into the fire that never shall be quenched:]{.wj} 
@46 [Where their worm dieth not, and the fire is not quenched.]{.wj} 
@47 [And if thine eye offend thee, pluck it out: it is better for thee to enter into the kingdom of God with one eye, than having two eyes to be cast into hell fire:]{.wj} 
@48 [Where their worm dieth not, and the fire is not quenched.]{.wj} 
@49 [For every one shall be salted with fire, and every sacrifice shall be salted with salt.]{.wj} 
@50 [Salt is good: but if the salt have lost his saltness, wherewith will ye season it? Have salt in yourselves, and have peace one with another.]{.wj}

# 12 
@1 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he began]{strong="G756" x-morph="robinson: V-ADI-3S 2"} [to speak]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 6"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"} [by]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [parables]{strong="G3850" x-morph="robinson: N-DPF 5"}. [A certain man planted a vineyard, and set an hedge about it, and digged a place for the winefat, and built a tower, and let it out to husbandmen, and went into a far country.]{.wj} 
@2 [And at the season he sent to the husbandmen a servant, that he might receive from the husbandmen of the fruit of the vineyard.]{.wj} 
@3 [And they caught him, and beat him, and sent him away empty.]{.wj} 
@4 [And again he sent unto them another servant; and at him they cast stones, and wounded him in the head, and sent him away shamefully handled.]{.wj} 
@5 [And again he sent another; and him they killed, and many others; beating some, and killing some.]{.wj} 
@6 [Having yet therefore one son, his wellbeloved, he sent him also last unto them, saying, They will reverence my son.]{.wj} 
@7 [But those husbandmen said among themselves, This is the heir; come, let us kill him, and the inheritance shall be ours.]{.wj} 
@8 [And they took him, and killed him, and cast him out of the vineyard.]{.wj} 
@9 [What shall therefore the lord of the vineyard do? he will come and destroy the husbandmen, and will give the vineyard unto others.]{.wj} 
@10 [And have ye not read this scripture; The stone which the builders rejected is become the head of the corner:]{.wj} 
@11 [This was the Lord’s doing, and it is marvellous in our eyes?]{.wj}

@33 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [to love]{strong="G25" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 3"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 4"} [with]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 5"} [all]{strong="G3650" x-morph="robinson: A-GSF 6"} [the heart]{strong="G3588 G2588" x-morph="robinson: T-GSF robinson: N-GSF 7 8"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 9"} [with]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 10"} [all]{strong="G3650" x-morph="robinson: A-GSF 11"} [the understanding]{strong="G3588 G4907" x-morph="robinson: T-GSF robinson: N-GSF 12 13"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 14"} [with]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 15"} [all]{strong="G3650" x-morph="robinson: A-GSF 16"} [the soul]{strong="G3588 G5590" x-morph="robinson: T-GSF robinson: N-GSF 17 18"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 19"} [with]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 20"} [all]{strong="G3650" x-morph="robinson: A-GSF 21"} [the strength]{strong="G3588 G2479" x-morph="robinson: T-GSF robinson: N-GSF 22 23"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 24"} [to love]{strong="G25" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 26"} his [neighbour]{strong="G4139" x-morph="robinson: ADV 28"} [as]{strong="G5613" x-morph="robinson: ADV 29"} [himself]{strong="G1438" x-morph="robinson: F-3ASM 30"}, [is]{strong="G2076" x-morph="robinson: V-PXI-3S 32"} [more]{strong="G4119" x-morph="robinson: A-NSN-C 31"} [than all]{strong="G3956" x-morph="robinson: A-GPN 33"} [whole burnt offerings]{strong="G3588 G3646" x-morph="robinson: T-GPN robinson: N-GPN 34 35"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 36"} [sacrifices]{strong="G3588 G2378" x-morph="robinson: T-GPF robinson: N-GPF 37 38"}. 
@34 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [when Jesus]{strong="G3588 G2424" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 2 3"} [saw]{strong="G1492" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAP-NSM 4"} [that]{strong="G3754" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 6"} [he answered]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-ADI-3S 8"} [discreetly]{strong="G3562" x-morph="robinson: ADV 7"}, [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 9"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 10"}, [Thou art not far from the kingdom of God.]{.wj} [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 19"} [no man]{strong="G3762" x-morph="robinson: A-NSM 20"} [after that]{strong="G3765" x-morph="robinson: ADV 21"} [durst]{strong="G5111" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 22"} [ask]{strong="G1905" x-morph="robinson: V-AAN 24"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 23"} any question. 

@35 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [Jesus]{strong="G3588 G2424" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 3 4"} [answered]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-AOP-NSM 2"} [and said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 5"}, [while he taught]{strong="G1321" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 6"} [in]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 7"} [the temple]{strong="G3588 G2411" x-morph="robinson: T-DSN robinson: N-DSN 8 9"}, [How say the scribes that Christ is the Son of David?]{.wj} 
@36 [For David himself said by the Holy Ghost, The LORD said to my Lord, Sit thou on my right hand, till I make thine enemies thy footstool.]{.wj} 
@37 [David therefore himself calleth him Lord; and whence is he then his son? And the common people heard him gladly.]{.wj} 

@38 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"} [in]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 7"} [doctrine]{strong="G3588 G1322" x-morph="robinson: T-DSF robinson: N-DSF 5 6"}, [Beware of the scribes, which love to go in long clothing, and love salutations in the marketplaces,]{.wj} 
@39 [And the chief seats in the synagogues, and the uppermost rooms at feasts:]{.wj} 
@40 [Which devour widows’ houses, and for a pretence make long prayers: these shall receive greater damnation.]{.wj} 

@41 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [Jesus]{strong="G3588 G2424" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 3 4"} [sat]{strong="G2523" x-morph="robinson: V-AAP-NSM 2"} [over against]{strong="G2713" x-morph="robinson: ADV 5"} [the treasury]{strong="G3588 G1049" x-morph="robinson: T-GSN robinson: N-GSN 6 7"}, [and beheld]{strong="G2334" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 8"} [how]{strong="G4459" x-morph="robinson: ADV 9"} [the people]{strong="G3588 G3793" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 10 11"} [cast]{strong="G906" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3S 12"} [money]{strong="G5475" x-morph="robinson: N-ASM 13"} [into]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 14"} [the treasury]{strong="G3588 G1049" x-morph="robinson: T-ASN robinson: N-ASN 15 16"}: [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 17"} [many]{strong="G4183" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 18"} [that were rich]{strong="G4145" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 19"} [cast]{strong="G906" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3P 20"} [in much]{strong="G4183" x-morph="robinson: A-APN 21"}. 
@42 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [there came]{strong="G2064" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAP-NSF 2"} [a certain]{strong="G1520" x-morph="robinson: A-NSF 3"} [poor]{strong="G4434" x-morph="robinson: A-NSF 5"} [widow]{strong="G5503" x-morph="robinson: N-NSF 4"}, [and she threw in]{strong="G906" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 6"} [two]{strong="G1417" x-morph="robinson: A-NUI 8"} [mites]{strong="G3016" x-morph="robinson: N-APN 7"}, [which]{strong="G3739" x-morph="robinson: R-NSN 9"} [make]{strong="G2076" x-morph="robinson: V-PXI-3S 10"} [a farthing]{strong="G2835" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 11"}. 
@43 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he called]{strong="G4341" x-morph="robinson: V-ADP-NSM 2"} unto him [his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 5"} [disciples]{strong="G3588 G3101" x-morph="robinson: T-APM robinson: N-APM 3 4"}, [and saith]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3S 6"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 7"}, [Verily I say unto you, That this poor widow hath cast more in, than all they which have cast into the treasury:]{.wj} 
@44 [For all they did cast in of their abundance; but she of her want did cast in all that she had, even all her living.]{.wj} 

# 13 
@1 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [as he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 3"} [went out]{strong="G1607" x-morph="robinson: V-PNP-GSM 2"} [of]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [the temple]{strong="G3588 G2411" x-morph="robinson: T-GSN robinson: N-GSN 5 6"}, [one]{strong="G1520" x-morph="robinson: A-NSM 9"} [of his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 12"} [disciples]{strong="G3588 G3101" x-morph="robinson: T-GPM robinson: N-GPM 10 11"} [saith]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3S 7"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 8"}, [Master]{strong="G1320" x-morph="robinson: N-VSM 13"}, [see]{strong="G1492" x-morph="robinson: V-AAM-2S 14"} [what manner of]{strong="G4217" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 15"} [stones]{strong="G3037" x-morph="robinson: N-NPM 16"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 17"} [what]{strong="G4217" x-morph="robinson: A-NPF 18"} [buildings]{strong="G3619" x-morph="robinson: N-NPF 19"} are here! 
@2 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [Jesus]{strong="G3588 G2424" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 2 3"} [answering]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-AOP-NSM 4"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 5"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 6"}, [Seest thou these great buildings? there shall not be left one stone upon another, that shall not be thrown down.]{.wj} 
@3 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [as he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 3"} [sat]{strong="G2521" x-morph="robinson: V-PNP-GSM 2"} [upon]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [the mount]{strong="G3588 G3735" x-morph="robinson: T-ASN robinson: N-ASN 5 6"} [of Olives]{strong="G3588 G1636" x-morph="robinson: T-GPF robinson: N-GPF 7 8"} [over against]{strong="G2713" x-morph="robinson: ADV 9"} [the temple]{strong="G3588 G2411" x-morph="robinson: T-GSN robinson: N-GSN 10 11"}, [Peter]{strong="G4074" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 16"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 17"} [James]{strong="G2385" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 18"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 19"} [John]{strong="G2491" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 20"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 21"} [Andrew]{strong="G406" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 22"} [asked]{strong="G1905" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3P 12"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 13"} [privately]{strong="G2398" x-morph="robinson: A-ASF 15"}, 
@4 [Tell]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAM-2S 1"} [us]{strong="G2254" x-morph="robinson: P-1DP 2"}, [when]{strong="G4219" x-morph="robinson: PRT-I 3"} [shall]{strong="G2071" x-morph="robinson: V-FXI-3S 5"} [these things]{strong="G5023" x-morph="robinson: D-NPN 4"} [be]{strong="G2071" x-morph="robinson: V-FXI-3S 5"}? [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 6"} [what]{strong="G5101" x-morph="robinson: I-NSN 7"} shall be [the sign]{strong="G3588 G4592" x-morph="robinson: T-NSN robinson: N-NSN 8 9"} [when]{strong="G3752" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 10"} [all]{strong="G3956" x-morph="robinson: A-APN 12"} [these things]{strong="G5023" x-morph="robinson: D-APN 13"} [shall be fulfilled]{strong="G4931" x-morph="robinson: V-PPN 14"}? 
@5 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [Jesus]{strong="G2424" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 3"} [answering]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-AOP-NSM 4"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 5"} [began]{strong="G756" x-morph="robinson: V-ADI-3S 6"} [to say]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 7"}, [Take heed lest any man deceive you:]{.wj} 
@6 [For many shall come in my name, saying, I am Christ; and shall deceive many.]{.wj} 
@7 [And when ye shall hear of wars and rumours of wars, be ye not troubled: for such things must needs be; but the end shall not be yet.]{.wj} 
@8 [For nation shall rise against nation, and kingdom against kingdom: and there shall be earthquakes in divers places, and there shall be famines and troubles: these are the beginnings of sorrows.]{.wj} 

@9 [But take heed to yourselves: for they shall deliver you up to councils; and in the synagogues ye shall be beaten: and ye shall be brought before rulers and kings for my sake, for a testimony against them.]{.wj} 
@10 [And the gospel must first be published among all nations.]{.wj} 
@11 [But when they shall lead you, and deliver you up, take no thought beforehand what ye shall speak, neither do ye premeditate: but whatsoever shall be given you in that hour, that speak ye: for it is not ye that speak, but the Holy Ghost.]{.wj} 
@12 [Now the brother shall betray the brother to death, and the father the son; and children shall rise up against their parents, and shall cause them to be put to death.]{.wj} 
@13 [And ye shall be hated of all men for my name’s sake: but he that shall endure unto the end, the same shall be saved.]{.wj} 

@14 [But when ye shall see the abomination of desolation, spoken of by Daniel the prophet, standing where it ought not, (let him that readeth understand,) then let them that be in Judæa flee to the mountains:]{.wj} 
@15 [And let him that is on the housetop not go down into the house, neither enter therein, to take any thing out of his house:]{.wj} 
@16 [And let him that is in the field not turn back again for to take up his garment.]{.wj} 
@17 [But woe to them that are with child, and to them that give suck in those days!]{.wj} 
@18 [And pray ye that your flight be not in the winter.]{.wj} 
@19 [For in those days shall be affliction, such as was not from the beginning of the creation which God created unto this time, neither shall be.]{.wj} 
@20 [And except that the Lord had shortened those days, no flesh should be saved: but for the elect’s sake, whom he hath chosen, he hath shortened the days.]{.wj} 
@21 [And then if any man shall say to you, Lo, here is Christ; or, lo, he is there; believe him not:]{.wj} 
@22 [For false Christs and false prophets shall rise, and shall shew signs and wonders, to seduce, if it were possible, even the elect.]{.wj} 
@23 [But take ye heed: behold, I have foretold you all things.]{.wj} 

@24 [But in those days, after that tribulation, the sun shall be darkened, and the moon shall not give her light,]{.wj} 
@25 [And the stars of heaven shall fall, and the powers that are in heaven shall be shaken.]{.wj} 
@26 [And then shall they see the Son of man coming in the clouds with great power and glory.]{.wj} 
@27 [And then shall he send his angels, and shall gather together his elect from the four winds, from the uttermost part of the earth to the uttermost part of heaven.]{.wj} 
@28 [Now learn a parable of the fig tree; When her branch is yet tender, and putteth forth leaves, ye know that summer is near:]{.wj} 
@29 [So ye in like manner, when ye shall see these things come to pass, know that it is nigh, even at the doors.]{.wj} 
@30 [Verily I say unto you, that this generation shall not pass, till all these things be done.]{.wj} 
@31 [Heaven and earth shall pass away: but my words shall not pass away.]{.wj} 

@32 [But of that day and that hour knoweth no man, no, not the angels which are in heaven, neither the Son, but the Father.]{.wj} 
@33 [Take ye heed, watch and pray: for ye know not when the time is.]{.wj} 
@34 [For the Son of man is as a man taking a far journey, who left his house, and gave authority to his servants, and to every man his work, and commanded the porter to watch.]{.wj} 
@35 [Watch ye therefore: for ye know not when the master of the house cometh, at even, or at midnight, or at the cockcrowing, or in the morning:]{.wj} 
@36 [Lest coming suddenly he find you sleeping.]{.wj} 
@37 [And what I say unto you I say unto all, Watch.]{.wj}

[And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 12"} [they murmured against]{strong="G1690" x-morph="robinson: V-INI-3P 13"} [her]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSF 14"}. 
@6 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [Jesus]{strong="G2424" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 3"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 4"}, [Let her alone; why trouble ye her? she hath wrought a good work on me.]{.wj} 
@7 [For ye have the poor with you always, and whensoever ye will ye may do them good: but me ye have not always.]{.wj} 
@8 [She hath done what she could: she is come aforehand to anoint my body to the burying.]{.wj} 
@9 [Verily I say unto you, Wheresoever this gospel shall be preached throughout the whole world, this also that she hath done shall be spoken of for a memorial of her.]{.wj}

@18 [They shall take up serpents; and if they drink any deadly thing, it shall not hurt them; they shall lay hands on the sick, and they shall recover.]{.wj} 

@19 [So]{strong="G3767" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 3"} [then]{strong="G3303" x-morph="robinson: PRT 2"} [after]{strong="G3326" x-morph="robinson: PREP 5"} [the Lord]{strong="G2962" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 4"} [had spoken]{strong="G2980" x-morph="robinson: V-AAN 7"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 8"}, [he was received up]{strong="G353" x-morph="robinson: V-API-3S 9"} [into]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 10"} [heaven]{strong="G3588 G3772" x-morph="robinson: T-ASM robinson: N-ASM 11 12"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 13"} [sat]{strong="G2523" x-morph="robinson: V-AAI-3S 14"} [on]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 15"} [the right hand]{strong="G1188" x-morph="robinson: A-GPM 16"} [of God]{strong="G3588 G2316" x-morph="robinson: T-GSM robinson: N-GSM 17 18"}. 
@20 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [they]{strong="G1565" x-morph="robinson: D-NPM 1"} [went forth]{strong="G1831" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAP-NPM 3"}, [and preached]{strong="G2784" x-morph="robinson: V-AAI-3P 4"} [every where]{strong="G3837" x-morph="robinson: ADV 5"}, [the Lord]{strong="G3588 G2962" x-morph="robinson: T-GSM robinson: N-GSM 6 7"} [working with]{strong="G4903" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-GSM 8"} them, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 9"} [confirming]{strong="G950" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-GSM 12"} [the word]{strong="G3588 G3056" x-morph="robinson: T-ASM robinson: N-ASM 10 11"} [with]{strong="G1223" x-morph="robinson: PREP 13"} [signs]{strong="G4592" x-morph="robinson: N-GPN 16"} [following]{strong="G1872" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-GPN 15"}.
